- **miette**: Stop on *. Two-Eyed balance: measure the last assistant message and block an output that shipped as one eye. Stop only — a subagent's final text is a return value to another agent, not an output addressed to a human, and holding it to a reader-accountability floor would corrupt structured returns. - **mod**: UserPromptSubmit. Hybrid: the classic UserPromptSubmit hook runs everywhere; the module loads only where function hooks are on (Claude Code &gt;= 2.1.287, or CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_FUNCTION_HOOKS=1 from 2.1.277). While the module runs it sets MY_MOD_ACTIVE, and the classic hook does nothing, so no event fires twice. ([ruvnet/ruflo](https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo/blob/HEAD/plugins/ruflo-plugin-creator/templates/mod/hooks/hooks.json) · ★82k)
